Let's consider optimization using active-set linear system H_w a = 1 but solved via multiplicative updates. This may be easier and powerful. We can implement a dual-primal algorithm:
```
a = normalize(parent)
for iterations:
  c=conv(a,a); M=max(c); score=...
  # weights w on lags: softmax(β c) or active within tol
  w = (c**p) / sum
  # compute gradient b = H_w a via FFT
  b = convolve(w, a_rev segment) * 2
  # update a multiplicatively to make b constant (for positive a)
  # Choose target b_target = median(b) or sum? We want reduce score.
  # Update in log: z=log a; step = -η (log b - mean(log b))? Since ln a change makes relative change.
  # If b_i large, a_i should decrease; if small, increase. So a *= exp(-η*(log b - mean log b)).
  # Line search over η maybe.
```
This is essentially natural gradient for F_w. It preserves positivity. It may converge to stationary point. New compared to previous: weights w depend on c and step equalizes gradient rather than just gradient descent with line search. We can tune β, p, η.

Can we connect to exact KKT? At optimum, b_i constant. This update drives that. It also handles boundaries via multiplicative (zeros stay? If a_i=0, log undefined; use small floor).

Let's test conceptually with uniform a, c triangular. w softmax high center? b_i=H_w a likely high where? Multiplicative update may shape.

We can use this as a local optimizer with decreasing β (start broad, then focus on peaks). For β large, w on max lags; stationarity with active weights. This is like previous active gradient but using multiplicative natural step. Could be faster/more robust.

Additionally, we can use "dual weights" optimization: The best w for current a can be found by choosing w argmax c; for KKT, any mixture of active max lags. Starting with w uniform over near-max. Then update a. Then update w. This is block coordinate descent on game. We can also perform "mirror descent" with averaging.

Potential algorithm timeline:
1. Initialize best sequences: parent, inverse-sqrt, maybe random/prof. Choose best by quick score.
2. Run dual-multiplicative optimizer for several settings (β schedules) to improve.
3. Run L-BFGS / active polish as before for local ref.

Let's outline final program:
```
"""
Dual natural-gradient active-set optimizer for minimizing 2n max conv(a,a)/sum^2.
Uses parent, inverse-sqrt initialization, multiplicative updates driven by
active-set Hankel gradients, L-BFGS log-sum-exp polish, and stochastic ILS.
"""
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

global t_start

# helpers: next_pow2, full_convolve, score, normalize, softmax, make_profile, invsqrt_profile

# hankel_apply
# active_weights (inverse gap)
# dual_polish(a_init, deadline, params)
# lbfgs_smooth as previous (maybe keep)
# stochastic ils using dual_perturbations

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
   set t_start, deadline
   best = parent or invsqrt
   phases:
     Phase1: quick multi-start: parent, invsqrt, maybe parent with edge modifications. Use quick_optimize (dual + lbfgs short) with moderate budget each.
     Phase2: intensive dual polish on best (schedule tol, eta) until time.
     Phase3: L-BFGS logsumexp with betas.
     Phase4: more dual polish.
     Phase5: ILS perturbations (global log noise, block, mirror) followed by quick dual+lbfgs.
     Phase6: multi-n resampling (n candidates) with dual polish.
     Phase7: final ultra-fine dual + lbfgs.
   return list floats clipped.
```

def _dual_polish(a_init, deadline, max_iters=None, tol_start=0.2, tol_end=0.005, eta_start=0.1, eta_end=0.001, p=1.0, min_time=5):
    a = normalize(a_init)
    best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)
    c=conv(a,a); M=...
    tol=tol_start; eta=eta_start; p_cur=p
    no_improve=0
    while time < deadline:
       # weights
       if tol > 0: active = c >= M*(1-tol); if active.sum()==0: argmax
       # inverse-gap: denom = M - c[active] + eps; w_active=1/denom
       # maybe raise to p: w_active = (1/denom)**p
       w=zeros; w[active]=...
       w/=sum
       b = hankel_apply(w,a)
       # If b has nonpositive, floor = max(min positive, 1e-30)
       logb=log(max(b,eps)); logb-=mean
       # Try eta values [eta, eta*0.5]
       improved=False
       for factor in [1,0.5,0.25]:
          step=eta*factor
          a_try = softmax(log(a)+? actually a * exp(-step logb) then normalize)
          # Use loga = log(a) - step logb; normalize exp
          sc_try=score(a_try)
          if sc_try < current_sc - 1e-13: accept; break
       if not improved: ... adapt eta, tol, restart best if stall
       else: update current_sc, best...
       # update c,M after accept; if not accept, no need? For weights next maybe still update with current a (unchanged) but tol changed.
       # decay tol: if improved, tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.99); eta=min(eta_start, eta*1.01)
       # if no improve, tol=max(...*0.9), eta*=0.7
       if no_improve large: a=best, reset tol/eta
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Detailed helper implementations:
```
def _next_pow2(n):
    k=1
    while k<n: k <<=1
    return max(2,k)

def _full_convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); out=n+m-1
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:out]
```
For self convolve:
```
def _self_convolve(a):
    n=len(a); out=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:out]
    return r
```
For hankel apply:
```
def _hankel_apply(w,a):
    # w length 2n-1, a length n
    ar=a[::-1]
    # full conv length len(w)+n-1 =3n-2?
    # Use _full_convolve
    c=_full_convolve(w,ar)
    n=len(a)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```

So slice [n-1 : 2*n-1] gives length n (0:n). For n=3, 2:5 -> indices 2,3,4. Good. Use `return c[n-1:2*n-1]`.

def _score(a):
    n=len(a); s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return np.inf
    c=_self_convolve(a)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max()/(s*s))
```

Dual polish implementation detailed:
```
def _make_weights(c, M, tol_frac, power, rng=None):
    n2=len(c)
    w=np.zeros(n2)
    if M <=0: w[0]=1; return w
    thresh = M*(1.0 - tol_frac)
    active = c >= thresh
    if not active.any(): active[np.argmax(c)]=True
    denom = M - c[active] + 1e-12*M
    w_ = 1.0 / denom
    w_ = w_ ** power
    s=w_.sum()
    if s<=0: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
    else: w[active]=w_/s
    return w
```
Maybe include some weight on non-active to avoid zeros? We can add small floor: w_all = c + eps; but focusing active good. Could combine: w = (1-alpha)*active_power + alpha * softmax(c)? Not necessary.

`_dual_step`:
```
def _dual_propose(a, c, M, eta, tol_frac, power, seed_std=1.0):
    n=len(a)
    w=_make_weights(c,M,tol_frac,power)
    b=_hankel_apply(w,a)
    # b can have negative? w,a nonnegative => b nonnegative; numerical >=0.
    b_floor = max(1e-300, 1e-12 * (b.sum()/n))
    b = np.maximum(b,b_floor)
    logb = np.log(b)
    logb = logb - logb.mean()
    std = logb.std()
    if std > 1e-12: logb = logb / std
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    # shift loga to prevent under/overflow
    z = loga - eta*logb
    z = z - z.max()
    a_new = np.exp(z)
    a_new /= a_new.sum()
    return a_new
```
Potential scale: eta after std normalization. If z difference large, exp okay after shift.

def _dual_polish(a_init, deadline, tol_start=0.25, tol_end=0.002, eta_start=0.25, eta_end=0.005, power=1.5, min_time_remaining=1.0):
   a=_normalize(a_init); c=_self_convolve(a); M=c.max(); cur=2*n*M
   best_a=a; best_sc=cur
   tol=tol_start; eta=eta_start; pow=power; no=0; stall=0
   while time + min_time < deadline:
      w=...
      b=...
      # maybe if b std too small, break
      accept=False
      for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1]:
          a_try=_dual_propose_from_b(a,c,M,b? we can recompute? if b fixed, use b with eta*factor)
          sc_try=_score(a_try)
          if sc_try < cur - 1e-13:
             accept=True; break
      if accept:
         a=a_try; c=_self_convolve(a); M=c.max(); cur=2*n*M
         if cur<best_sc: best...
         no=0; eta=min(eta_start, eta*1.02); tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.995); pow maybe increase slowly
      else:
         no+=1
         eta*=0.75
         if no % 10 ==0: tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.85)
         if no >= 200:
            a=best_a; c=...; cur=best_sc; tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.5); eta=eta_start*0.5; no=0
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def _smooth_obj(z, beta, n):
   a=_softmax(z)
   c=_self_convolve(a)
   cmax=c.max()
   shifted=(c-cmax)/beta
   e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-700,0))
   s=e.sum()
   if not finite or s<...: return 2*n*cmax
   return 2*n*(cmax+beta*np.log(s))
```

`_smooth_grad`:
```
def _smooth_grad(z,beta,n):
   a=_softmax(z)
   c=_self_convolve(a)
   shifted=c/beta
   shifted-=shifted.max()
   e=np.exp(shifted)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<...: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1
   else: p=e/s
   ar=a[::-1]
   pc=_full_convolve(p, ar)
   h=pc[n-1:2*n-1]
   W=float(np.dot(p,c))
   return 4.0*n*(h - a*W)
```
Note p length 2n-1. h_j =∑_k p_k a_{k-j}; good.

Let's refine dual polish for performance and robustness:
```
def _dual_polish(a_init, deadline, tol_start=0.25, tol_end=0.002, eta_start=0.12, power=1.5, floor_frac=1e-6, w_update=25, min_time_remaining=1.0, verbose=False):
    global T_START
    n=len(a_init)
    a=_normalize(a_init.copy())
    c=_self_convolve(a)
    M=float(c.max()); cur=2.0*n*M
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=cur
    tol=tol_start; eta=eta_start; p=power
    w=None; w_count=0; no=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - min_time_remaining:
        if w is None or w_count >= w_update:
            w=_make_weights(c,M,tol,p,floor_frac)
            w_count=0
        b=_hankel_apply(w,a)
        # if b variance tiny, maybe break or change w
        zvals=log...
        # line search: factors = (1,0.5,0.25)
        accept=False; a_best_local=a; sc_best_local=cur
        for factor in factors:
           a_try=_apply_update(a,b,eta*factor)
           sc_try=_score(a_try)
           if sc_try < sc_best_local - 1e-14:
               sc_best_local=sc_try; a_best_local=a_try
        if sc_best_local < cur - 1e-14:
           a=a_best_local; cur=sc_best_local
           c=_self_convolve(a); M=float(c.max())
           no=0
           if cur < best_sc - 1e-14: best...
           # adapt: eta up if many improve, tol down slowly
           eta = min(0.5, eta*1.03)
           tol = max(tol_end, tol*0.995)
        else:
           no += 1
           eta *= 0.7
           if no % 5 == 0:
               tol = max(tol_end, tol*0.9)
               p = min(8.0, p*1.2)
           if no >= 100:
               a=best_a.copy(); c=_self_convolve(a); M=...; cur=best_sc
               eta=min(0.5, eta*2.0); tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.5); p=power; no=0
        w_count += 1
    return best_a, best_sc
```
Need `_make_weights` with floor. If c has zeros and M small, active includes all? If tol large (1.0?), all. Use `tol_start` maybe 0.5 for broad. If `tol=0.5`, active c≥0.5M. For smooth c many. Good. If tol too close to1, threshold negative -> all. We'll clamp tol in [0.001,0.999].

`_apply_update`: use `np.log(a + a_max*1e-300?)` Actually if a has zeros, log(0) -inf. Use floor `a_floor = np.finfo(float).tiny` maybe. But multiplying by floor in normalization negligible. `a_safe = np.where(a > 1e-300, a, 1e-300)`.
Compute `logb` standardized. If `std` too small, skip update (return a). If eta*factor ==0 skip.
```
def _apply_update(a,b,eta):
    if eta <= 0: return a
    b_safe=np.maximum(b,1e-300)
    logb=np.log(b_safe)
    logb=logb-logb.mean()
    std=logb.std()
    if std < 1e-12: return a
    logb /= std
    a_safe=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
    z=np.log(a_safe) - eta*logb
    z=z-z.max()
    out=np.exp(z)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return a
    return out/s
```
But `log(a_safe)` each time O(n); okay. We could precompute loga in loop; but after updates changes. Fine.

L-BFGS implementation:
```
def _run_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=5.0, betas='auto', max_iter_per_beta=400):
   n=len(a_init)
   c=_self_convolve(a_init); cmax=...
   # beta schedule
   c_sorted=np.sort(c)[::-1]
   gap=c_sorted[0]-c_sorted[min(64,len(c)-1)]
   if gap < cmax*0.002: gap=cmax*0.002
   base=max(gap, cmax*0.01)
   betas=base*np.array([...])
   best_a=a_init.copy(); best_sc=_score(best_a)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a_init,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
   for beta in betas:
      if time > deadline - min_time_remaining: break
      remaining=...
      max_iter=min(max_iter_per_beta, max(30, int(remaining/0.05)))? 
      res=minimize(_smooth_obj, z, args=(beta,n), jac=_smooth_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':max_iter,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxcor':50})
      a_new=_softmax(res.x); sc=_score(a_new)
      if sc<best_sc: update z
   return best_a
```

def _quick_optimize(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=5.0, do_lbfgs=True, lbfgs_betas=3):
    global T_START
    a, sc = _dual_polish(a_init, min(deadline - (10 if do_lbfgs else 0), t+10), ... limited)
    if do_lbfgs and time < deadline - min_time_remaining:

    return a, sc

Now, multi-start profiles. Let's implement:
```
def _profile_invsqrt(n):
   w=np.empty(n)
   w[0]=1.0
   if n>1:
      k=np.arange(n-1, dtype=float)
      ratios=(2*k+1)/(2*k+2)
      w[1:]=np.cumprod(ratios)
   return normalize(w)
```
Check ratio for k=0: 1/2; w1=0.5 (C2,1/4=0.5). Good.

- linear: 1 - 0.5*t? to taper end.

Resample:
```
def _resample(a,new_n):
   old_n=len(a)
   if old_n==new_n: return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   a_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return normalize(a_new)
```
This preserves endpoints; if endpoint zeros maybe okay.

L-BFGS `maxiter`: p increase. We can run a function `_pnorm_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, p_values)`.
```
def _pnorm_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=5, p_values=None):
   best_a=a_init; best_sc=score
   z=log(a)-mean
   for p in p_values:
      if time > deadline-min_time: break
      remaining=...
      max_iter = min(600, max(30, int(remaining / 0.1)))? 
      # maybe scale down for large p? p high less smooth, use 150.
      res=minimize(_pnorm_obj, z, args=(p,n), jac=_pnorm_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options=...)
      a_new=softmax(res.x)
      sc=score(a_new)
      if sc<best_sc: best_a=a_new; z=log(a_new)-mean
   return best_a
```
Use `ftol=1e-15, gtol=1e-12, maxcor=50`. `maxls=50`.

Let's detail `_pnorm_obj` robust:
```
def _pnorm_obj(z, p, n):
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _self_convolve(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    cs = c / M
    logcs = np.log(np.maximum(cs, 1e-300))
    # mean(cs^p)
    vals = np.exp(np.clip(p * logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    mean_val = float(vals.mean())
    if mean_val <= 0.0:
        return 2.0 * n * M
    logF = np.log(2.0 * n) + np.log(M) + np.log(mean_val) / p
    return float(np.exp(logF))
```
Since mean_val<=1, logF maybe log(2n)+logM+... M~1/n, F~O1. safe.

`_pnorm_grad`:
```
def _pnorm_grad(z,p,n):
    a=_softmax(z)
    c=_self_convolve(a)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    M=float(c.max())
    if M<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    cs=c/M
    logcs=np.log(np.maximum(cs,1e-300))
    if p==1:
       r=np.ones_like(cs)
    else:
       r=np.exp(np.clip((p-1)*logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    Rsum=float(r.sum())
    if Rsum <= 0:
       w=np.ones_like(cs)/len(cs)
    else:
       w=r/Rsum
    vals=np.exp(np.clip(p*logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    mean_val=float(vals.mean())
    if mean_val <=0: mean_val=1e-300
    # C = 4n * Rsum / (L * mean_val^{(p-1)/p}) if Rsum is sum cs^{p-1}, L=len(c)
    L=len(c)
    logC = np.log(4.0*n) + np.log(max(Rsum,1e-300)) - np.log(L) - ((p-1)/p)*np.log(mean_val)
    C = float(np.exp(min(50.0, logC))) # cap to avoid overflow; gradient scale not too huge
    # But if cap, L-BFGS maybe. For n=24576, logC ~ log(98288) ~11.5, fine. No cap needed but safe 700.
    h=_hankel_apply(w,a)
    R_total=float(np.dot(w,c))
    return C*(h - a*R_total)
```

Let's write `_pnorm_lbfgs` with parameter `max_iter_per=300`.
```
def _pnorm_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=5.0, p_values=None, max_iter_per=300):
   if p_values is None: p_values=[8,16,32]
   best_a=...; best_sc=...
   z=...
   for p in p_values:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - min_time_remaining: break
      remaining=deadline - min_time_remaining - time.monotonic()
      if remaining < 3: break
      max_iter = min(max_iter_per, max(30, int(remaining / 0.1)))

def _perturb_global(a, sigma, rng):
   n=len(a)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   z = z + sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
   z = z - z.mean()
   return _softmax(z)

Block:
```
def _perturb_block(a, sigma, rng):
  n=len(a); block=max(16,n//8)
  start=int(rng.integers(0,n-block))
  a_p=a.copy()
  z=np.log(np.maximum(a_p[start:start+block],1e-300))
  z += sigma*rng.standard_normal(block)
  a_p[start:start+block]=np.exp(z - np.max(z))
  return _normalize(a_p)
```

Let's write `_make_weights_power`:
```
def _make_weights_power(c, q):
    M=float(c.max())
    L=len(c)
    if M <= 0.0:
       return np.ones(L)/L
    cs=c/M
    if q <= 0.0:
       w=np.ones(L)
    else:
       logcs=np.log(np.maximum(cs, 1e-300))
       w=np.exp(np.clip(q*logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    w += 1e-10
    s=w.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): w=np.ones(L)
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
For q large, many zeros but max 1.均匀 floor.

def _dual_polish(a_init, deadline, power_start=1.0, power_end=12.0, eta_start=1.0, eta_end=0.05, min_time_remaining=1.0, line_trials=3, w_update=20):
   n=len(a_init); a=_normalize(a_init)
   c=_self_convolve(a); M=...; cur=2n*M; best...
   start=time.monotonic(); total=max(1.0, deadline-start)
   power=power_start; eta=eta_start; no=0; w=None; w_count=0
   factors = (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1) if line_trials==4? We can just use 3.
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - min_time_remaining:
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
       frac=min(1.0, elapsed/total)
       power = power_start + (power_end-power_start)*(frac**0.6)
       if w is None or w_count>=w_update or not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
          w=_make_weights_power(c, power)
          w_count=0
       b=_hankel_apply(w,a)
       R=float(np.dot(w,c))
       if R<=0 or not finite: w=None; continue
       # try etas
       a_best=a; sc_best=cur
       for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
          a_try=_apply_update(a,b,eta*factor,R)
          c_try=_self_convolve(a_try)
          sc_try=2.0*n*float(c_try.max())
          if sc_try < sc_best - 1e-14:
             sc_best=sc_try; a_best=a_try; c_best=c_try
       if sc_best < cur - 1e-14:
          a=a_best; c=c_best; M=float(c.max()); cur=sc_best; no=0
          if cur<best_sc-1e-14: best...
          eta=min(4.0, eta*1.03)
       else:
          no +=1; eta=max(eta_end, eta*0.7)
          if no % 20 ==0:
             # occasionally broaden power: if power > power_start + ...
             pass
          if no >= 150:
             a=best_a.copy(); c=_self_convolve(a); M=...; cur=best_sc; eta=min(4.0, eta_start); no=0; # reset power? set start? Could set a quick? We can set `start = time.monotonic()` to reset annealing? Maybe not.
       w_count +=1
   return best_a,best_sc

`_apply_update` with R:
```
def _apply_update(a,b,eta,R):
    if eta<=0 or R<=0: return a
    rel = b / R - 1.0
    rel = np.clip(rel, -3.0, 3.0)
    out = a * np.exp(-eta * rel)
    s=out.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return a
    return out/s
```
This can make large coordinates? rel clipped. Good.

```
def _softmax(z):
   z=z - np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-700,700))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
   return e/s
```

def _basic_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=3.0, p_values=(8,), max_iter=100, dual_seconds=None, do_lbfgs=True):
   if dual_seconds is None: dual_seconds = min(10.0, max(2.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.5))
   end_dual = min(deadline - (5 if do_lbfgs else 0), time.monotonic()+dual_seconds)
   a,sc = _dual_polish(a_init, end_dual, power_start=1.0, power_end=8.0, eta_start=1.0, line_trials=3, min_time_remaining=1.0)
   if do_lbfgs and time.monotonic() < deadline - min_time_remaining:
      a2,sc2 = _pnorm_lbfgs(a, min(deadline-min_time_remaining, time.monotonic()+max(5.0,(deadline-time.monotonic())*0.4)), p_values=p_values, max_iter_per=max_iter, min_time_remaining=min_time_remaining)
      if sc2 < sc: a=a2; sc=sc2
   return a,sc
```

   attempt=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - min_time_remaining:

       if kind=='global': a_pert=_perturb_global(current,sigma,rng)

       elif sc_opt < current_sc - 1e-12:
           current=a_opt; current_sc=sc_opt
       attempt +=1
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Time per iteration ~7s; 500s ~70 iterations. Good.

Could the ILS quick dual line search scoring 3 candidates each, dual time 4s maybe 20 iterations? okay.

Now, `_pnorm_lbfgs` if `max_iter_per` small and remaining large, but p loop may consume more than expected. We cannot interrupt. For ILS, max_iter=60, p=16: 60 gradients ~0.5s, okay.

Now, one subtle issue: In `_pnorm_grad`, `h = _hankel_apply(w, a)` uses w normalized over all lags. Then `R_total = dot(w, c)`. In gradient formula, h = weighted average of a (not gradient factor). F_a? For p-norm, gradient wrt a is `K * 2?` times h, but scale C computed assuming gradient direction h - R. We omitted factor 2? Let's check C formula: We set C = 4n * Rsum / (L mean^{(p-1)/p}). Then grad_z = C a (h - R). Did we include factor 2? Earlier F_a = C h with C=4n * S/(L A^{(p-1)/p}). Let's verify for p=2 active uniform: F=2n sqrt(mean(c^2)). If all c=M, F=2nM. But if all c=M, p-norm gradient should equal max gradient in flat vector? Since objective scale invariant and symmetric. Formula gave C=4n, grad_z=a*C*(h-R). For all c=M, R=M? ∑w c=M. h? Is h-R zero? Not necessarily; gradient wrt z may be zero if a stationary? For uniform a, c triangular not all equal. If what sequence has all c=M, none. Hard.
Need ensure scale factor includes 2 correctly. Derive F_a scalar again:
F = 2n A^{1/p}, A = (1/L)Σc^p.
dF/da_i = 2n*(1/p) A^{1/p-1} * (p/L) Σ c_k^{p-1} ∂c_k/∂a_i = 2n/L * A^{1/p-1} Σ c_k^{p-1} (2 b_i) where b_i=Σ w unnormalized? Let r_k=c^{p-1}. Let S=Σr. Define h_i = (1/S)Σ r_k a_{k-i} (using ∂c/2). Then Σ r (2b_i)=2S h_i. Thus F_a = (4n S / L) A^{(1-p)/p} h_i.
A = M^p mean_cs^p. A^{(1-p)/p}=M^{1-p} mean_cs^{1-p}. S=M^{p-1} Rsum (Rsum=Σcs^{p-1}). Product S A^{(1-p)/p}=Rsum * mean_cs^{1-p}. Thus scalar C = 4n/L * Rsum * mean_cs^{1-p}.
Earlier I had C=4n Rsum/(L mean^{(p-1)/p}) = same because mean^{1-p}=mean^{-(p-1)}. Good.
For p→∞, S~active M^{p-1}, A=M^p, C=4n. h with w among active. F_a=4n h. For max gradient, if active weighted uniform, ∂max =2h, F=2n max => F_a=4n h. Good. So C correct.
Then softmax gradient = a_i(F_a_i - ∑ a_j F_a_j) = C a_i (h_i - ∑a h). ∑a h=S? =Σw c=R_total. So grad=C a(h-R). Correct. Good.

```python
"""
Dual natural-gradient optimizer for flattening the self-convolution of a
nonnegative sequence.

The main objective after normalizing sum(a)=1 is to minimize
    2*n*max_k conv(a,a,k).

The method combines:
  - inverse-square-root and parent-sequence initializations,
  - p-norm surrogate objectives with p increasing toward infinity,
  - a multiplicative dual "power iteration" in which lag weights are
    proportional to c_k^q (q = p-1), and the sequence update tries to
    equalize the corresponding Hankel gradient,
  - L-BFGS on softmax variables for the p-norm surrogate,
  - stochastic iterated local search for escaping local minima,
  - limited multi-length exploration.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(n):
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)


def _full_convolve(a, b):
    n = len(a)
    m = len(b)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    out_n = n + m - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:out_n]


def _self_convolve(a):
    n = len(a)
    out_n = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:out_n]


def _score(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = _self_convolve(a)
    cmax = max(0.0, float(c.max()))
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def _score_normalized(a):
    # Assumes sum(a) is already 1 (or close enough)
    n = len(a)
    c = _self_convolve(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(c.max())))


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-30:
        return np.ones(len(a), dtype=float) / max(1, len(a))
    return a / s


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(len(z), dtype=float) / max(1, len(z))
    return e / s


def _resample(a, new_n):
    old_n = len(a)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    a_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize(a_new)


def _profile_invsqrt(n):
    w = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    w[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        k = np.arange(n - 1, dtype=float)
        ratios = (2.0 * k + 1.0) / (2.0 * k + 2.0)
        w[1:] = np.cumprod(ratios)
    return _normalize(w)


def _profile_power(n, p, offset=0.5):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + offset
    a = np.power(i, -p)
    return _normalize(a)


def _profile_invsqrt_taper(n):
    a = _profile_invsqrt(n)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    win = 1.0 - 0.45 * t
    return _normalize(a * win)


def _hankel_apply(w, a):
    # b_j = sum_{i=0}^{n-1} w[j+i] a[i]
    n = len(a)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([w[0] * a[0]], dtype=float)
    ar = a[::-1]
    c = _full_convolve(w, ar)
    return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _make_weights_power(c, q):
    L = len(c)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=float) / L
    cs = c / M
    if q <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)
    else:
        logcs = np.log(np.maximum(cs, 1e-300))
        w = np.exp(np.clip(q * logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    w = w + 1e-10
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def _apply_dual_update(a, b, eta, R):
    if eta <= 0.0 or R <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(R):
        return a
    rel = b / R - 1.0
    rel = np.clip(rel, -3.0, 3.0)
    out = a * np.exp(-eta * rel)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a
    return out / s


def _dual_polish(a_init, deadline,
                 power_start=1.0, power_end=12.0,
                 eta_start=1.0, eta_end=0.05,
                 line_trials=3, w_update=20,
                 min_time_remaining=1.0):
    n = len(a_init)
    a = _normalize(a_init.copy())
    c = _self_convolve(a)
    current_sc = _score_normalized(a)
    if not np.isfinite(current_sc):
        return a, np.inf

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc

    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(deadline - start))
    eta = eta_start
    no_improve = 0
    w = None
    w_count = 0
    factors = [1.0, 0.5, 0.25][:max(1, int(line_trials))]

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - min_time_remaining:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / total)
        power = power_start + (power_end - power_start) * (frac ** 0.65)

        if w is None or w_count >= w_update:
            w = _make_weights_power(c, power)
            w_count = 0

        b = _hankel_apply(w, a)
        R = float(np.dot(w, c))
        if not np.isfinite(R) or R <= 1e-300:
            w = None
            continue

        a_best = a
        c_best = c
        sc_best = current_sc

        for factor in factors:
            et = eta * factor
            if et <= 0.0:
                continue
            a_try = _apply_dual_update(a, b, et, R)
            if np.array_equal(a_try, a):
                continue
            c_try = _self_convolve(a_try)
            sc_try = _score_normalized(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < sc_best - 1e-14:
                sc_best = sc_try
                a_best = a_try
                c_best = c_try

        if sc_best < current_sc - 1e-14:
            a = a_best
            c = c_best
            current_sc = sc_best
            no_improve = 0
            eta = min(4.0, eta * 1.035)
            if current_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = current_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eta = max(eta_end, eta * 0.72)
            if no_improve >= 160:
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = _self_convolve(a)
                current_sc = best_sc
                eta = min(4.0, max(eta_start, eta_end * 10.0))
                no_improve = 0
                w = None

        w_count += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def _pnorm_obj(z, p, n):
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _self_convolve(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return 0.0
    cs = c / M
    logcs = np.log(np.maximum(cs, 1e-300))
    vals = np.exp(np.clip(p * logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    mean_val = float(vals.mean())
    if mean_val <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mean_val):
        return float(2.0 * n * M)
    logF = np.log(2.0 * n) + np.log(M) + np.log(mean_val) / p
    return float(np.exp(logF))


def _pnorm_grad(z, p, n):
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _self_convolve(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    cs = c / M
    logcs = np.log(np.maximum(cs, 1e-300))

    if p <= 1.0:
        r = np.ones_like(cs)
    else:
        r = np.exp(np.clip((p - 1.0) * logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    Rsum = float(r.sum())
    if Rsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Rsum):
        w = np.ones_like(cs) / len(cs)
    else:
        w = r / Rsum

    vals = np.exp(np.clip(p * logcs, -700.0, 0.0))
    mean_val = float(vals.mean())
    if mean_val <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mean_val):
        mean_val = 1e-300

    L = len(c)
    logC = (np.log(4.0 * n)
            + np.log(max(Rsum, 1e-300))
            - np.log(L)
            - ((p - 1.0) / max(1.0, p)) * np.log(mean_val))
    C = float(np.exp(min(700.0, max(-700.0, logC))))
    if not np.isfinite(C):
        C = 1.0

    h = _hankel_apply(w, a)
    Rtot = float(np.dot(w, c))
    return C * a * (h - Rtot)


def _pnorm_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=3.0,
                 p_values=None, max_iter_per=300):
    if p_values is None:
        p_values = [8.0, 16.0, 32.0]

    a = _normalize(a_init.copy())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)

    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_a, best_sc

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - z.mean()

    for p in p_values:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - min_time_remaining:
            break
        remaining = deadline - min_time_remaining - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 2.0:
            break

        max_iter = int(min(max_iter_per, max(30, remaining / 0.12)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _pnorm_obj, z,
                args=(float(p), len(a)),
                jac=_pnorm_grad,
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                options={
                    'maxiter': max_iter,
                    'ftol': 1e-16,
                    'gtol': 1e-12,
                    'maxcor': 50,
                    'maxls': 50,
                }
            )
            a_new = _softmax(res.x)
            sc_new = _score(a_new)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                z = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
                z = z - z.mean()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_a, best_sc


def _perturb_global(a, sigma, rng):
    n = len(a)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
    z = z - z.mean()
    return _softmax(z)


def _perturb_block(a, sigma, rng):
    n = len(a)
    block = max(16, n // 8)
    if n <= block:
        return _perturb_global(a, sigma, rng)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - block))
    a_p = a.copy()
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a_p[start:start + block], 1e-300))
    z = z + sigma * rng.standard_normal(block)
    z = z - z.max()
    a_p[start:start + block] = np.exp(z)
    return _normalize(a_p)


def _perturb_sparse(a, sigma, rng):
    n = len(a)
    k = max(8, n // 12)
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
    a_p = a.copy()
    a_p[idx] *= np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(k))
    return _normalize(a_p)


def _perturb_mirror(a, sigma, rng):
    n = len(a)
    seg = max(8, n // 16)
    if n <= seg:
        return _perturb_global(a, sigma, rng)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - seg))
    a_p = a.copy()
    a_p[start:start + seg] = a_p[start:start + seg][::-1]
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a_p, 1e-300))
    z = z + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    z = z - z.mean()
    return _softmax(z)


def _quick_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=2.0,
                  dual_seconds=5.0, p_values=(16.0,), max_iter=80):
    t = time.monotonic()
    dual_end = min(deadline - min_time_remaining, t + max(1.0, dual_seconds))
    a, sc = _dual_polish(
        a_init, dual_end,
        power_start=1.5, power_end=10.0,
        eta_start=0.9, line_trials=3,
        min_time_remaining=0.4
    )
    if time.monotonic() + 1.5 < deadline - min_time_remaining and len(p_values) > 0:
        lb_end = min(deadline - min_time_remaining, time.monotonic() + max(1.5, 0.45 * dual_seconds))
        a2, sc2 = _pnorm_lbfgs(
            a, lb_end,
            min_time_remaining=0.3,
            p_values=list(p_values),
            max_iter_per=max_iter
        )
        if sc2 < sc:
            a, sc = a2, sc2
    return a, sc


def _stochastic_ils(a_init, deadline, seed=7, min_time_remaining=35.0):
    current = _normalize(a_init.copy())
    current_sc = _score(current)
    best_a = current.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2 ** 63 - 1))

    strategies = [
        ('global', 0.015),
        ('block', 0.025),
        ('sparse', 0.07),
        ('global', 0.035),
        ('mirror', 0.02),
        ('block', 0.04),
        ('sparse', 0.12),
        ('global', 0.008),
    ]

    attempt = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - min_time_remaining:
        kind, sigma = strategies[attempt % len(strategies)]
        if kind == 'global':
            a_pert = _perturb_global(current, sigma, rng)
        elif kind == 'block':
            a_pert = _perturb_block(current, sigma, rng)
        elif kind == 'sparse':
            a_pert = _perturb_sparse(current, sigma, rng)
        elif kind == 'mirror':
            a_pert = _perturb_mirror(current, sigma, rng)
        else:
            a_pert = current.copy()

        a_opt, sc_opt = _quick_polish(
            a_pert,
            deadline - min_time_remaining,
            min_time_remaining=0.5,
            dual_seconds=4.0,
            p_values=(16.0,),
            max_iter=55
        )

        if np.isfinite(sc_opt):
            if sc_opt < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
                current = a_opt.copy()
                current_sc = sc_opt
                print(f"    ILS new best: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic():.1f}s")
            elif sc_opt < current_sc - 1e-12:
                current = a_opt.copy()
                current_sc = sc_opt

        attempt += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def _load_parent():
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if raw.size == 0:
        return None
    raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(raw.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return None
    raw = raw / s
    if raw.size > 65536:
        raw = _resample(raw, 49152)
    return raw


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 18.0)

    parent = _load_parent()
    if parent is None:
        n_main = 24576
        parent = _profile_invsqrt(n_main)
    else:
        n_main = len(parent)

    best_a = _normalize(parent.copy())
    best_sc = _score(best_a)
    print(f"initial best: score={best_sc:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    # ---------- Phase 1: diverse multi-start ----------
    ms_total = min(280.0, max(80.0, (deadline - t0) * 0.14))
    ms_deadline = t0 + ms_total
    starts = [
        ('parent', _normalize(parent.copy())),
        ('invsqrt', _profile_invsqrt(n_main)),
        ('invsqrt_taper', _profile_invsqrt_taper(n_main)),
        ('power_045', _profile_power(n_main, 0.45)),
    ]
    per_start = max(25.0, ms_total / max(1, len(starts)) - 10.0)

    for name, a0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 800.0:
            break
        a_opt, sc_opt = _quick_polish(
            a0,
            min(ms_deadline, time.monotonic() + per_start),
            min_time_remaining=5.0,
            dual_seconds=max(6.0, 0.55 * per_start),
            p_values=(8.0),
            max_iter=90
        )
        print(f"  multi-start {name}: {sc_opt:.10f}")
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print(f"  NEW BEST: {best_sc:.10f} [{name}] t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    # ---------- Phase 2: broad dual refinement ----------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 650.0:
        dual_end = min(deadline - 650.0, time.monotonic() + 420.0)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _dual_polish(
            best_a, dual_end,
            power_start=0.4, power_end=5.0,
            eta_start=1.0, line_trials=4,
            w_update=25, min_time_remaining=3.0
        )
        print(f"  broad dual: {sc_opt:.10f}")
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print(f"  NEW BEST: {best_sc:.10f} [broad_dual] t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    # ---------- Phase 3: p-norm L-BFGS ----------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 500.0:
        lb_end = min(deadline - 500.0, time.monotonic() + 260.0)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _pnorm_lbfgs(
            best_a, lb_end,
            min_time_remaining=4.0,
            p_values=[4.0, 8.0, 16.0, 32.0],
            max_iter_per=320
        )
        print(f"  lbfgs1: {sc_opt:.10f}")
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print(f"  NEW BEST: {best_sc:.10f} [lbfgs1] t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    # ---------- Phase 4: fine dual refinement ----------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 400.0:
        dual_end = min(deadline - 400.0, time.monotonic() + 320.0)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _dual_polish(
            best_a, dual_end,
            power_start=4.0, power_end=25.0,
            eta_start=0.45, line_trials=4,
            w_update=30, min_time_remaining=3.0
        )
        print(f"  fine dual: {sc_opt:.10f}")
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print(f"  NEW BEST: {best_sc:.10f} [fine_dual] t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    # ---------- Phase 5: stochastic ILS ----------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 260.0:
        ils_end = min(deadline - 260.0, time.monotonic() + 500.0)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _stochastic_ils(
            best_a, ils_end,
            seed=int(seed) + 11,
            min_time_remaining=40.0
        )
        print(f"  ils: {sc_opt:.10f}")
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print(f"  NEW BEST: {best_sc:.10f} [ils] t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    # ---------- Phase 6: limited multi-length exploration ----------
    n_candidates = [n_main, 32768, 40960, 20480, 16384]
    seen = set()
    n_unique = []
    for nn in n_candidates:
        if nn not in seen and nn >= 1024:
            seen.add(nn)
            n_unique.append(int(nn))

    for nn in n_unique:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 150.0:
            break
        a_init = _resample(best_a, nn)
        sc_init = _score(a_init)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _quick_polish(
            a_init,
            min(deadline - 150.0, time.monotonic() + 75.0),
            min_time_remaining=4.0,
            dual_seconds=35.0,
            p_values=(16.0, 32.0),
            max_iter=180
        )
        print(f"  multi-n n={nn}: init={sc_init:.10f} opt={sc_opt:.10f}")
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print(f"  NEW BEST: {best_sc:.10f} [n={nn}] t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    # ---------- Phase 7: final ultra-fine polish ----------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        dual_end = min(deadline - 55.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _dual_polish(
            best_a, dual_end,
            power_start=8.0, power_end=60.0,
            eta_start=0.2, line_trials=4,
            w_update=40, min_time_remaining=2.0
        )
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        a_opt, sc_opt = _pnorm_lbfgs(
            best_a,
            deadline - 8.0,
            min_time_remaining=2.0,
            p_values=[32.0, 64.0, 128.0],
            max_iter_per=450
        )
        if sc_opt < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()

        # Tiny final dual sweep if any time left
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
            a_opt, sc_opt = _dual_polish(
                best_a,
                deadline - 2.0,
                power_start=20.0, power_end=120.0,
                eta_start=0.08, line_trials=3,
                w_update=30, min_time_remaining=0.2
            )
            if sc_opt < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()

    final = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        final = _profile_invsqrt(24576)
        s = float(final.sum())
    final = final / s
    final_score = _score(final)
    print(f"FINAL: score={final_score:.10f}, n={len(final)}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
